# GLM corroborating review — PR #207 finance slice, round 13 (same-family, not the Sol gate)

**Verdict: PASS-with-nits.** The round-12 fix is correct and complete for its stated scope, and I found no regression in the four focus areas. Five low-severity nits below, none of which reproduces the round-12 phantom-record/inflation defect class with a realistic input. The Sol re-run is still owed.

## Focus 1 — round-12 fix, `registrable_domain` (`src/finance_mt_probe.py`)

**Holds.** I traced the guard at `src/finance_mt_probe.py:272-280` against a hostile battery by hand:

- **Empty labels are fully covered.** An empty label can only appear as a leading dot or two consecutive dots (a trailing dot is stripped in `_canonical_host` before the check, and the check runs *after* the second `_canonical_host` call, so percent-encoded forms `%2Ecom` / `a%2E%2Eb.com` decode *before* the `startswith(".")`/`".."` test). `".com"`, `"a..b"`, `".example.com"`, `"a...b"`, `"...com"`, `"com..net"`, `"..tr"`, `"./"`, `"...."` all → `"unknown"`.
- **Trailing dot still heals**: `https://example.com./p` → `example.com` (hostname's trailing dot stripped at `finance_mt_probe.py:229`, then normal two-label logic). Pinned by `test_registrable_domain_empty_label_forms`.
- **Single-label keeps the metadata fallback**: `https://com./x` → `"com"` (no dot) → `collect_gdelt` falls back to the `domain` metadata at `finance_mt_probe.py:452-453`.
- **IDN still canonicalises**: `faß.de` → `xn--fa-hia.de` via UTS-46; idna-absent and undecodable hosts return `""` → `"unknown"` (never an IDNA-2003 ß→ss split).
- **No legitimate form broken**: leading-dot and consecutive-dot hosts are invalid DNS, so nothing valid is newly rejected. IPv6 literals, brackets, port-only, `user:pass@` with no host, spaces, ill-typed input, `""` — all `"unknown"`.
- **Integration is right**: `"unknown"` contains no dot, so the metadata fallback runs; if metadata is also unusable the article is malformed *before* the exact-dedup check (`finance_mt_probe.py:454-470`), so repeats inflate `malformed`, never `deduped_exact` — exactly the pinned test.

## Focus 2 — `collect_gdelt` pipeline

**Holds.** Locatability (URL host → metadata fallback) precedes dedup; malformed counting is honest per-occurrence; story keys are NFKC + lowercase + whitespace-collapse, full length, punctuation-preserving, with empty titles keyed by URL so they never over-merge; a zero-evidence source returns `status="failed"` with an error naming all four counts (`finance_mt_probe.py:522-533`).

## Focus 3 — `finance_ft_runner.py` (v0.4)

**Holds.** The exact digest-pinned reference is enforced in `verify_config`, `build_docker_argv`, and both snapshot delegation argvs (`finance_ft_runner.py:585-616`); `dry_run is not True` rejection; spot-only; credential-field rejection; `add_config_files` + `--extra` overlay rejection duplicated at builder level; the sha-bound entrypoint script contains no shell metacharacters (config_name and digest are grammar-bound) and passes freqtrade args as positional params; snapshot write uses `set -C` + O_EXCL + inode/nlink/byte round-trip assertions; stop trailer (with prior-output digest) is appended after `docker stop` on both the success and ready-timeout paths, and stop rc≠0 fails the cycle. The `crashed_early` path returns without `docker rm`, but that branch fires only on "No such container" — there is nothing to remove; an exited-but-present container flows to the ready-timeout path which does `rm -f`.

## Focus 4 — `finance_goals.py`

**Holds.** `_receipt_blockers` is a true fail-closed wrapper (any exception → blocker, never a lost response); newest-run-governs with unparseable timestamps sorted newest; verifier identity is exact list membership against `independent_verifiers` (`finance_goals.py:513-520`), so `"glm-codex"` cannot ride on `"codex"`; run and verification receipts both require artifacts; verification must cite an exact `(sha256, path)` pair of the run and not predate it; dependency satisfaction is recursion-safe with a cycle guard and cross-program deps included; unattributable receipts lock every goal.

## Findings (all low severity — none blocks)

1. **Low / `src/finance_mt_probe.py:281-296`** — Junk-label dotted hosts still become a healthy record's source: `registrable_domain("https://1.2/x")` → `"1.2"`, `"https://a.-/x"` → `"a.-"`, `"https://-.-/x"` → `"-.-"`. These pass the `"." not in domain` check at `finance_mt_probe.py:452`, so (absent metadata) such an article produces a healthy record citing a pseudo-domain — the same *class* as round-12 finding 1, but with non-empty, individually non-resolvable labels (no TLD `2`; `-` is not a valid DNS label). Failure scenario: a GDELT feed entry with url `https://1.2/story` yields `gdelt-doc-2.0:1.2` as a counted distinct publisher. No dedup-inflation synergy (distinct URLs dedup once by URL only). Plausibility is very low; a last-label-must-be-alphabetic guard would close it.
2. **Nit / `src/finance_mt_probe.py:294-296`** — IPv4 literals collapse to their last two octets (`192.168.0.1` → `"0.1"`), a mislabelled but locatable source; distinct IPs remain distinct, so only cosmetics.
3. **Nit / `src/finance_mt_probe.py:230-237`** — IDN + trailing dot (`https://bücher.de./x`) does not heal: `idna.encode` rejects the trailing dot → `""` → `"unknown"`, while the ASCII spelling heals. Conservative direction only (under-count, never phantom).
4. **Nit / `src/finance_goals.py:272-276`** — the `repo:` artifact prefix does `repo_root / art_path[5:]` without confining the result to `repo_root`; a receipt can name `repo:../../any/file` and have it hashed. No content is disclosed (only missing/mismatch verdicts) and receipts are committed evidence, but resolving then checking `resolve().is_relative_to(repo_root)` would be tidier.
5. **Nit / `src/finance_goals.py:99-103`** — a corrupt `runtime-evaluation.json` is silently swallowed and defaults applied. Defaults *are* the strict standing rules so no gate can weaken, but it is an outlier against the module's fail-closed posture.

**Observation (possibly adjudicated in earlier rounds; runner unchanged this round):** the sha-bound entrypoint protects the *config* bytes, but freqtrade also loads strategy `.py` files from the same host-writable `user_data` mount (`--userdir /freqtrade/user_data`), so a workspace writer can still swap strategy code between verification and exec. In dry-run with no credentials the blast radius is minimal; noting it only for completeness.

## Statement of lane

This is a **same-family GLM corroborating read, not the independent gate**. Dynamic execution was blocked in this lane, so the hostile-input battery was hand-traced statically rather than run; the two round-12 pinned tests were verified by manual execution against the code as written. **The Sol (gpt-6-sol-class Codex) round-13 re-review remains owed after quota returns and should not be considered discharged by this pass.**

---
title: Aoteru model-host routing contract
status: target-contract
owner: odysseus
as_of: 2026-08-20
scope: host placement, model routing, token economy, verification, continuous improvement
---

# Aoteru model-host routing contract

## Aim

Provide one central, provider-neutral routing authority for Aoteru so the user can speak to one persistent conversational front end while Odysseus selects the cheapest adequate execution route across deterministic tools, local models, Codex models and Claude models, on the correct worker host, with explicit verification and bounded escalation.

The contract must optimise quality, token/quota efficiency, latency and locality without fragmenting authority across skills, repositories, hosts or model-specific wrappers.

Target path:

```text
user -> Aoteru conversational front end -> Odysseus task envelope
     -> authority/repo -> eligible host -> cheapest adequate mechanism/model
     -> deterministic verification -> bounded escalation/independent review
     -> compact result -> Aoteru response
```

The conversational model owns intent and communication. Odysseus owns routing. Workers are disposable execution resources.

## Invariants

1. One central routing authority in Odysseus. No repo, skill, MCP or worker may create a competing model-selection or host-selection policy.
2. Resolve authority/repository before execution host; resolve execution host before model.
3. Deterministic software precedes model inference whenever adequate.
4. Adequate local inference precedes paid inference when measured quality meets the task quality floor.
5. Local-first is not local-at-any-cost: paid escalation is required when the local route does not meet the quality or verification requirement.
6. Routing uses stable capability aliases and live measurements, not permanent vendor/model rankings in business logic.
7. Home and lab are first-class worker targets. Current unavailability must not be hard-coded into architecture.
8. Today, lab is the only verified/available worker. Home remains registered but ineligible until live evidence sets verified, healthy and reachable true.
9. The laptop/interface is the human control surface, not a normal execution worker.
10. Repo mutation requires the existing Odysseus parking/lease authority. Routing never widens write authority.
11. Workers receive bounded task/evidence pointers, not whole conversational transcripts or duplicated repository context.
12. Verification is deterministic first, then independent-model review only when required.
13. Escalation is evidence-triggered, not based on vague difficulty judgments.
14. No swarm by default. One worker owns each task unit; parallelism is justified only by independent evidence gathering or deliberately independent verification.
15. Routing telemetry may improve future choices but cannot silently weaken permissions, domain gates, quality floors or verification requirements.
16. Subscription model versions are not economic routing knobs; when a subscription lane is chosen, resolve the newest eligible premium model at dispatch and allow only reasoning effort to vary. Fail closed on missing freshness/eligibility evidence. See `docs/subscription-model-currency-policy.md`.

## Human-facing default

The normal laptop Claude harness should present Aoteru through a strong, efficient conversational model. The current preferred role is Claude Sonnet-class inference at a moderate effort setting, but this is a role rather than a permanent model pin.

Aoteru should normally submit work to Odysseus instead of executing heavy repo/research work in the conversational context merely because the front-end model can do it.

## Host model

```yaml
# config/estate.yaml
hosts:
  interface:
    role: controller
    identity_verified: true
    # No worker block: controllers are never execution workers.

  lab:
    role: worker
    identity_verified: true
    worker:
      enabled: true
      transport: local
      qualified_executors: [deterministic, local, codex, codex-write]

  home:
    role: worker
    identity_verified: true
    worker:
      enabled: false
      transport: ssh
      qualified_executors: []

# config/models.yaml
capabilities:
  - alias: local-fast
    binding: qwen3:8b
    qualified_hosts:
      hz2-workstation:
        evidence: docs/aoteru-lm4-production-canary-evidence.md
        # binding: optional host-specific override
```

Host identity, worker enablement, executor qualification, alias qualification
and live state are separate claims. `identity_verified` records a governed
identity check and grants no eligibility on its own. `worker.enabled` is the
operator's governed permission to route to the host, while
`worker.qualified_executors` records the evidence-backed executor set. Each
model alias independently names its `qualified_hosts`; an optional host-local
`binding` overrides the alias default.

An eligible host has role `lab` or `home`, verified identity, an enabled
worker and a healthy live worker response. An executable route additionally
requires its executor to be qualified and live, every requested alias to be
qualified and physically present on that host, any requested repository to
resolve there, and no conflicting live lease. Health, inventory and repository
availability are runtime observations, never inferred from old documentation.
During migration, legacy `verified` is identity evidence only when
`identity_verified` is absent; it never implies `worker.enabled`.

## Routing dimensions

Every model-required task resolves both:

```text
WHERE?                         WHAT?
verified worker host           deterministic mechanism
                               local-fast
                               local-strong
                               Luna-class
                               Haiku-class
                               Terra-class
                               Sonnet-class
                               Sol-class
                               Opus-class
```

A route is therefore an execution tuple, not only a model name:

```yaml
route:
  host: lab
  executor: local | codex | claude | deterministic
  model_alias: local-fast | local-strong | code-fast | code-strong | reasoning-strong | null
  concrete_model: resolved_from_live_registry
```

## Default economic ladder

Use only as a seed policy; P7/live benchmark evidence overrides it.

```text
0 deterministic software
1 local-fast
2 local-strong
3 Luna/Haiku class
4 Terra/Sonnet class
5 Sol class
6 Opus class
```

Luna and Haiku largely occupy cheap scout/verification roles. Prefer the provider already native to the active workflow unless cross-provider independence is required.

Terra-class inference is normally the routine implementation worker. Sonnet-class inference is normally the Aoteru conversational foreman and can perform orchestration/reasoning when justified.

Sol-class inference is the normal expensive escalation for difficult engineering, debugging, scientific reasoning and deep review. Opus-class inference is reserved for frontier ambiguity, long-horizon architecture/synthesis, high-consequence arbitration or independent review where its measured advantage justifies the quota cost.

## Model aliases

Business logic must target capability aliases rather than concrete model brands:

```yaml
aliases:
  local-fast:
    purpose: cheap extraction, classification, reconnaissance

  local-strong:
    purpose: strongest adequate local reasoning/implementation

  code-fast:
    purpose: routine code work

  code-strong:
    purpose: difficult code work

  reasoning-strong:
    purpose: difficult analysis/research/architecture

  vision:
    purpose: multimodal work

  embedding:
    purpose: derived semantic indexing

  reranker:

+    (tmp_path / "link.json").symlink_to(target)
+    escape = _receipt("MT-00", fg.KIND_RUN, run_id="run-esc", days_ago=0,
+                      name="escape-artifact.json",
+                      artifacts=[{
+                          "label": "esc",
+                          "sha256": "a" * 64,
+                          "path": f"repo:../{outside.name}/secret.txt",
+                      }])
+    link = _receipt("MT-00", fg.KIND_RUN, run_id="run-lnk", days_ago=0,
+                    name="link-artifact.json",
+                    artifacts=[{
+                        "label": "lnk",
+                        "sha256": "a" * 64,
+                        "path": "repo:link.json",
+                    }])
+    result = fg.evaluate_goals(
+        registries=[MT, FT],
+        receipts=_satisfying_mt00_receipts() + [
+            escape, link, _receipt("MT-00", fg.KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION),
+        ],
+        policy=fg.load_policy(), now=NOW, repo_root=tmp_path,
+    )
+    assert result["ok"] is True
+    mt00 = _goal(result, "MT-00")
+    assert mt00["satisfied"] is False
+    assert any("escapes the repository" in b for b in mt00["blockers"])
+    assert any("escape-artifact.json" in b for b in mt00["blockers"])
+    assert any("link-artifact.json" in b for b in mt00["blockers"])
+

+def test_junk_label_dotted_hosts_are_unknown():
+    # "1.2", "a.-", "-.-" contain a dot (so they pass the "."-check in
+    # collect_gdelt) but their TOP label is not alphabetic — a TLD is
+    # alphabetic (IDN TLDs are punycode "xn--…"), so these are the same
+    # phantom-record class as the round-12 dotted-labelless fix
+    for url in ("https://1.2/x", "https://a.-/x", "https://-.-/x"):
+        assert mp.registrable_domain(url) == "unknown", url
+
+
+def test_ipv4_literal_is_not_last_two_octets():
+    # 192.168.0.1 previously collapsed to the pseudo-domain "0.1"; a
+    # numeric top label makes an IPv4 literal publisher-unlocatable
+    assert mp.registrable_domain("https://192.168.0.1/x") == "unknown"
+    assert mp.registrable_domain("https://1.2.3.4/x") == "unknown"
+
+
+def test_numeric_sld_still_a_domain():
+    # numeric NON-top labels are legal: 888.com is a real registrable
+    # domain and must survive the TLD guard
+    assert mp.registrable_domain("https://888.com/story") == "888.com"
+    assert mp.registrable_domain("https://www.888.com/s") == "888.com"

+def test_junk_label_host_article_is_malformed():
+    # end-to-end: a GDELT entry with a dotted junk host is malformed on
+    # every occurrence — never a healthy record, never dedup inflation
+    payload = {"articles": [
+        {"url": "https://1.2/story", "title": "Junk"},
+        {"url": "https://1.2/story", "title": "Junk"},
+    ]}

+        "bitcoin", max_records=10, collected_at="2026-11-06T00:00:00Z",
+        first_seen_index={}, fetch=lambda url, timeout: payload,
+    )
+    assert result.status == "failed"
+    assert result.records == []
+    assert result.deduped_exact == 0
+    assert "malformed=2" in (result.error or "")

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Deterministic finance goal-lock evaluator (MT-xx / FT-xx).
     2	
     3	Task cards ``finance-market-media-intelligence-2026-10-29.md`` and
     4	``finance-paper-trading-goals-2026-10-29.md``; canonical goal registries
     5	live in ``tyecam1/misumi@main:config/finance-*-goals.json`` and are
     6	mirrored read-only into ``config/finance-goals/`` for the runtime.
     7	
     8	The registries are durable DEFINITIONS, not runtime state. This module
     9	derives current status on every call, fail-closed:
    10	
    11	- a goal is **satisfied** only while it has a ``run`` receipt with
    12	  status=pass and a ``run_id``, inside its TTL, whose recorded artifact
    13	  hashes still match the live files, AND a current
    14	  ``independent_verification`` receipt (status=pass, with its own
    15	  hash-checked artifacts) from an accepted independent verifier — matched
    16	  by EXACT identity, never substring — naming that exact run, AND every
    17	  dependency of the goal is itself satisfied (own evidence can never
    18	  unlock a goal whose dependencies are still locked);
    19	- a goal is **eligible** (may be worked on) when its unlock arm holds:
    20	  root goals need a current ``operator_attestation``; dependent goals
    21	  need every dependency satisfied;
    22	- anything absent, stale, future-dated, hash-mismatched, malformed or
    23	  verified only by a corroborating (non-independent) lane is a blocker —
    24	  locked, never assumed good. Evidence supersession is strict in BOTH
    25	  directions: the NEWEST run receipt governs (when it stops validating
    26	  the goal locks with no fallback to older runs), older receipts are
    27	  notes only, and a fresh run + a verification that cites one of the
    28	  run's own artifacts (exact sha256+path pair, dated at/after the run)
    29	  can re-satisfy the goal after the code or artifacts it cites change.
    30	  A receipt that cannot even be attributed to a goal (unparseable, no
    31	  goal_id) locks EVERY goal until the ledger is repaired.
    32	
    33	No scheduler, no writes, no network: a pure function of (registries,
    34	receipts, files, clock). The HTTP adapter in
    35	``routes/finance_goals_routes.py`` exposes it read-only.
    36	"""
    37	
    38	from __future__ import annotations
    39	
    40	import hashlib
    41	import json
    42	from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
    43	from pathlib import Path
    44	from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
    45	
    46	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    47	DEFAULT_REGISTRIES_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "config" / "finance-goals"
    48	DEFAULT_RECEIPTS_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "automation" / "review" / "finance" / "receipts"
    49	DEFAULT_POLICY_PATH = DEFAULT_REGISTRIES_DIR / "runtime-evaluation.json"
    50	
    51	KIND_RUN = "run"
    52	KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION = "operator_attestation"
    53	KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION = "independent_verification"
    54	VALID_STATUSES = {"pass", "fail", "insufficient"}
    55	
    56	DEFAULT_POLICY: Dict[str, Any] = {
    57	    "receipt_ttl_days": {
    58	        KIND_RUN: 7,
    59	        KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION: 365,
    60	        KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION: 30,
    61	    },
    62	    # Accepted independent verifier identities (standing rules: GLM lanes
    63	    # are corroborating only; Sol-class Codex or the human owner are the
    64	    # independent lanes that can discharge a gate).
    65	    "independent_verifiers": ["codex", "sol", "human:tye"],
    66	    "goal_ttl_overrides": {},
    67	}
    68	
    69	REQUIRED_RECEIPT_FIELDS = ("goal_id", "kind", "status", "observed_at")
    70	
    71	# A receipt dated more than this far in the FUTURE is rejected: clocks
    72	# drift, but a receipt that predates its own evaluation by months is
    73	# evidence tampering, not freshness.
    74	FUTURE_SKEW_MINUTES = 5
    75	
    76	
    77	class ReceiptError(ValueError):
    78	    """A receipt file is malformed — recorded as a blocker, never fatal."""
    79	
    80	
    81	def _parse_iso(value: str) -> datetime:
    82	    return datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    83	
    84	
    85	def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    86	    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    87	    with path.open("rb") as handle:
    88	        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(65536), b""):
    89	            digest.update(chunk)
    90	    return digest.hexdigest()
    91	
    92	
    93	def sha256_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
    94	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    95	
    96	
    97	def load_policy(path: Path = DEFAULT_POLICY_PATH) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    98	    policy = json.loads(json.dumps(DEFAULT_POLICY))
    99	    if path.exists():
   100	        try:
   101	            override = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   102	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   103	            return policy
   104	        for key in ("receipt_ttl_days", "independent_verifiers"):
   105	            if isinstance(override.get(key), dict):
   106	                policy[key].update(override[key])
   107	            elif isinstance(override.get(key), list):
   108	                policy[key] = override[key]
   109	        if isinstance(override.get("goal_ttl_overrides"), dict):
   110	            policy["goal_ttl_overrides"].update(override["goal_ttl_overrides"])
   111	    return policy
   112	
   113	
   114	def load_registries(directory: Path = DEFAULT_REGISTRIES_DIR) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   115	    registries = []
   116	    for path in sorted(directory.glob("finance-*-goals.json")):
   117	        try:
   118	            data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   119	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   120	            registries.append(
   121	                {"file": path.name, "program_id": path.stem, "error": str(exc),
   122	                 "goals": []}
   123	            )
   124	            continue
   125	        data["_file"] = path.name
   126	        data["_sha256"] = sha256_file(path)
   127	        registries.append(data)
   128	    return registries
   129	
   130	
   131	def load_receipts(directory: Path = DEFAULT_RECEIPTS_DIR) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   132	    receipts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   133	    if not directory.exists():
   134	        return receipts
   135	    for path in sorted(directory.glob("*.json")):
   136	        try:
   137	            data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   138	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   139	            receipts.append(
   140	                {"_file": path.name, "_invalid": f"unparseable: {exc}"}
   141	            )
   142	            continue
   143	        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   144	            receipts.append({"_file": path.name, "_invalid": "not an object"})
   145	            continue
   146	        # NEVER trust a receipt-supplied _file: the real file name is the
   147	        # ledger key downstream, and a spoofed one could collide with (and
   148	        # silently overwrite) another receipt's bucket entry
   149	        data["_file"] = path.name
   150	        # ill-TYPED values poison the same way missing ones do: a list
   151	        # goal_id/kind/status would otherwise raise mid-derivation and
   152	        # take the whole /goals response down instead of locking
   153	        missing = [
   154	            f for f in REQUIRED_RECEIPT_FIELDS
   155	            if not isinstance(data.get(f), str) or not data.get(f, "").strip()
   156	        ]
   157	        problems: List[str] = []
   158	        if missing:
   159	            problems.append(f"missing/ill-typed fields: {missing}")
   160	        elif data["status"] not in VALID_STATUSES:
   161	            problems.append(
   162	                f"status {data['status']!r} not in {sorted(VALID_STATUSES)}"
   163	            )
   164	        artifacts = data.get("artifacts")
   165	        if artifacts is not None and (
   166	            not isinstance(artifacts, list)
   167	            or any(not isinstance(a, dict) for a in artifacts)
   168	        ):
   169	            problems.append("artifacts must be a list of objects")
   170	        if problems:
   171	            data["_invalid"] = "; ".join(problems)
   172	        receipts.append(data)
   173	    return receipts
   174	
   175	
   176	def _ttl_days(receipt: Dict[str, Any], policy: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[int]:
   177	    goal_overrides = policy.get("goal_ttl_overrides") or {}
   178	    per_goal = (goal_overrides.get(receipt.get("goal_id")) or {}).get(receipt["kind"])
   179	    if isinstance(per_goal, int):
   180	        return per_goal
   181	    return (policy.get("receipt_ttl_days") or {}).get(receipt["kind"])
   182	
   183	
   184	def _receipt_blockers(
   185	    receipt: Dict[str, Any], *, now: datetime, policy: Dict[str, Any],
   186	    repo_root: Path, check_artifacts: bool,
   187	) -> List[str]:
   188	    """Fail-closed wrapper: ANY unexpected failure while evaluating a
   189	    hostile receipt is itself a blocker (the receipt cannot vouch for
   190	    anything) — it can never take the whole /goals response down. This
   191	    is deliberate: reviews repeatedly found new ill-typed-input crashes,
   192	    so the invariant is enforced structurally, not per-field."""
   193	    try:
   194	        return _receipt_blockers_inner(
   195	            receipt, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   196	            check_artifacts=check_artifacts,
   197	        )
   198	    except Exception as exc:  # broad by design: fail closed, never 500
   199	        return [
   200	            f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')!r}: could not be evaluated "
   201	            f"({type(exc).__name__}: {exc}) — locked"
   202	        ]
   203	
   204	
   205	def _receipt_blockers_inner(
   206	    receipt: Dict[str, Any], *, now: datetime, policy: Dict[str, Any],
   207	    repo_root: Path, check_artifacts: bool,
   208	) -> List[str]:
   209	    if receipt.get("_invalid"):
   210	        return [f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: {receipt['_invalid']}"]
   211	    blockers: List[str] = []
   212	    try:
   213	        observed = _parse_iso(receipt["observed_at"])
   214	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   215	        return [f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: observed_at not ISO-8601"]
   216	    if observed.tzinfo is None:
   217	        observed = observed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   218	    ttl = _ttl_days(receipt, policy)
   219	    if ttl is None:
   220	        blockers.append(
   221	            f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: unknown kind {receipt['kind']!r}"
   222	        )
   223	    else:
   224	        try:
   225	            expiry = observed + timedelta(days=ttl)
   226	        except OverflowError:
   227	            # 9999-12-31T23:59:59Z + ttl overflows datetime; a receipt
   228	            # stamped at the far future edge is future-dated, not a crash
   229	            expiry = None
   230	        if expiry is not None and now > expiry:
   231	            blockers.append(
   232	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: stale (observed "
   233	                f"{receipt['observed_at']}, ttl {ttl}d)"
   234	            )
   235	        elif expiry is None:
   236	            blockers.append(
   237	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: future-dated (observed "
   238	                f"{receipt['observed_at']} beyond datetime range; recovers "
   239	                "only if re-stamped within range)"
   240	            )
   241	        elif observed > now + timedelta(minutes=FUTURE_SKEW_MINUTES):
   242	            # fail-closed but not permanent: the receipt ages into
   243	            # validity once the clock reaches its stamped time, so a
   244	            # single mis-dated receipt can never lock a goal forever
   245	            blockers.append(
   246	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: future-dated (observed "
   247	                f"{receipt['observed_at']}, now "
   248	                f"{now.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()}; recovers when "
   249	                "the clock reaches the stamped time)"
   250	            )
   251	    if check_artifacts:
   252	        for artifact in receipt.get("artifacts") or []:
   253	            if not isinstance(artifact, dict):
   254	                blockers.append(
   255	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: malformed artifact entry"
   256	                )
   257	                continue
   258	            label = artifact.get("label")
   259	            recorded = artifact.get("sha256")
   260	            art_path = artifact.get("path")
   261	            if (
   262	                not isinstance(label, str) or not label
   263	                or not isinstance(recorded, str) or not recorded
   264	                or not isinstance(art_path, str) or not art_path
   265	            ):
   266	                # ill-typed (list/object/int) fields are malformed, not a
   267	                # crash: they must lock the goal, not lose the response
   268	                blockers.append(
   269	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: malformed artifact entry"
   270	                )
   271	                continue
   272	            if art_path.startswith("repo:"):
   273	                # a repo:-prefixed artifact must stay INSIDE the repo:
   274	                # resolve and confine, so a receipt can never point the
   275	                # verifier at arbitrary host files (a symlink planted
   276	                # inside the repo that resolves outside is confined away
   277	                # too). Only the hash verdict would leak, but confinement
   278	                # is the fail-closed posture (GLM corroborating read,
   279	                # round-13 finding 4).
   280	                root = Path(repo_root).resolve()
   281	                path = (repo_root / art_path[5:]).resolve()
   282	                if not path.is_relative_to(root):
   283	                    blockers.append(
   284	                        f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact path "
   285	                        f"escapes the repository: {art_path}"
   286	                    )
   287	                    continue
   288	            else:
   289	                path = Path(art_path)
   290	            if not path.exists():
   291	                blockers.append(
   292	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact missing: {art_path}"
   293	                )
   294	            elif not path.is_file():
   295	                # a well-typed path that names a DIRECTORY (or anything
   296	                # else non-regular) is a per-goal blocker, never an
   297	                # IsADirectoryError that loses the whole response
   298	                blockers.append(
   299	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact is not a "
   300	                    f"regular file: {art_path}"
   301	                )
   302	            else:
   303	                try:
   304	                    actual = sha256_file(path)
   305	                except OSError:
   306	                    blockers.append(
   307	                        f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact "
   308	                        f"unreadable: {art_path}"
   309	                    )
   310	                else:
   311	                    if actual != recorded:
   312	                        blockers.append(
   313	                            f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact hash "
   314	                            f"mismatch ({label})"
   315	                        )
   316	    return blockers
   317	
   318	
   319	def _artifact_check_needed(receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   320	    return receipt.get("kind") in (KIND_RUN, KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION)
   321	
   322	
   323	def evaluate_goals(
   324	    *,
   325	    registries: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
   326	    receipts: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
   327	    policy: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   328	    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
   329	    repo_root: Path = REPO_ROOT,
   330	) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   331	    registries = registries if registries is not None else load_registries()
   332	    receipts = receipts if receipts is not None else load_receipts()
   333	    policy = policy if policy is not None else load_policy()
   334	    now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   335	
   336	    goals: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
   337	    program_of: Dict[str, str] = {}
   338	    for registry in registries:
   339	        for goal in registry.get("goals") or []:
   340	            goals[goal["id"]] = {**goal, "_program": registry.get("program_id")}
   341	            program_of[goal["id"]] = registry.get("program_id") or "?"
   342	
   343	    by_goal: Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]]] = {
   344	        KIND_RUN: {},
   345	        KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION: {},
   346	        KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION: {},
   347	    }
   348	    # Malformed evidence is never invisible: receipts that carry a
   349	    # goal_id but cannot be classified lock that goal, and receipts that
   350	    # cannot even be attributed (unparseable, no goal_id) lock EVERY
   351	    # goal — a corrupted ledger must fail closed, not quietly drop the
   352	    # poisoned file out of the derivation.
   353	    malformed_by_goal: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
   354	    global_invalid: List[str] = []
   355	    for receipt in receipts:
   356	        file_name = receipt.get("_file") or "?"
   357	        raw_goal = receipt.get("goal_id")
   358	        goal_id = raw_goal.strip() if isinstance(raw_goal, str) else None
   359	        kind = receipt.get("kind")
   360	        invalid_reason = receipt.get("_invalid")
   361	        if goal_id is None or not goal_id:
   362	            # cannot be attributed to any goal at all (unparseable JSON,
   363	            # missing, EMPTY or ill-typed goal_id): poisons the whole
   364	            # ledger
   365	            global_invalid.append(
   366	                f"{file_name} ({invalid_reason or 'no usable goal_id'})"
   367	            )
   368	            continue
   369	        if goal_id not in goals:
   370	            # names a goal no loaded registry knows: it can neither be
   371	            # isolated to that goal nor safely ignored (the entry would
   372	            # vanish from the derivation) — lock everything
   373	            global_invalid.append(
   374	                f"{file_name} (names unknown goal {goal_id!r})"
   375	            )
   376	            continue
   377	        if not isinstance(kind, str) or kind not in by_goal or invalid_reason:
   378	            # attributed to a KNOWN goal but malformed (bad status,
   379	            # missing/ill-typed fields) or of unknown/missing/ill-typed
   380	            # kind: locks that goal specifically
   381	            malformed_by_goal.setdefault(goal_id, []).append(
   382	                f"{file_name} ("
   383	                + (invalid_reason or f"unusable kind {kind!r}")
   384	                + ")"
   385	            )
   386	            continue
   387	        by_goal[kind].setdefault(goal_id, {})[file_name] = receipt
   388	
   389	    evaluated: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   390	
   391	    def _observed_at(receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[datetime]:
   392	        """Parse observed_at, or None when unparseable (unorderable)."""
   393	        try:
   394	            parsed = _parse_iso(receipt["observed_at"])
   395	        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
   396	            return None
   397	        if parsed.tzinfo is None:
   398	            parsed = parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   399	        return parsed
   400	
   401	    def _order_key(receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
   402	        parsed = _observed_at(receipt)
   403	        if parsed is None:
   404	            # unparseable receipts sort LAST: an unreadable timestamp can
   405	            # never be proven older than the newest receipt, so it must
   406	            # never quietly sort to the back of history
   407	            return (1, datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc))
   408	        return (0, parsed)
   409	
   410	    def own_evidence_blockers(goal_id: str) -> List[str]:
   411	        """Blockers in this goal's OWN run/verification receipts.
   412	
   413	        The NEWEST run receipt (by observed_at) governs — valid or not.
   414	        If the newest run no longer validates (stale, future-dated,
   415	        artifact hash mismatch, unparseable timestamp), the goal is
   416	        LOCKED even when an older run still validates: fresh evidence
   417	        cannot silently fall back to a superseded result. Older receipts
   418	        are notes, surfaced only while the goal stays locked. A receipt
   419	        whose observed_at cannot be parsed is treated as newest (it
   420	        cannot be proven older), and a receipt that cannot be classified
   421	        at all locks the goal from the malformed bucket."""
   422	        malformed = malformed_by_goal.get(goal_id) or []
   423	        if malformed:
   424	            return [
   425	                f"{goal_id}: malformed receipt(s) in the evidence ledger — "
   426	                "locked: " + "; ".join(malformed)
   427	            ]
   428	
   429	        notes: List[str] = []
   430	        run_receipts = by_goal[KIND_RUN].get(goal_id) or {}
   431	        ordered = sorted(
   432	            run_receipts.items(),
   433	            key=lambda kv: (_order_key(kv[1]), kv[0]),
   434	        )
   435	        if not ordered:
   436	            return [f"{goal_id}: no run receipt"]
   437	
   438	        def run_problems(name: str, receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:
   439	            problems: List[str] = []
   440	            if receipt.get("status") != "pass":
   441	                problems.append(f"run receipt {name} status={receipt.get('status')!r}")
   442	            elif not isinstance(receipt.get("run_id"), str) or not receipt.get(
   443	                "run_id"
   444	            ):
   445	                # a non-string run_id (list/object) is unusable identity,
   446	                # and value-equality must never let an ill-typed pair
   447	                # match an ill-typed verification below
   448	                problems.append(f"run receipt {name} has no usable run_id")
   449	            elif not receipt.get("artifacts"):
   450	                problems.append(f"run receipt {name} records no artifacts")
   451	            else:
   452	                problems.extend(_receipt_blockers(
   453	                    receipt, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   454	                    check_artifacts=_artifact_check_needed(receipt),
   455	                ))
   456	            if _observed_at(receipt) is None:
   457	                problems.append(
   458	                    f"run receipt {name} has an unparseable observed_at — "
   459	                    "cannot be ordered against other evidence"
   460	                )
   461	            return problems
   462	
   463	        # Older receipts are history: their problems are noted but can
   464	        # neither lock nor unlock the goal. Only the NEWEST governs —
   465	        # and unparseable timestamps sort newest, so they lock.
   466	        for name, receipt in ordered[:-1]:
   467	            if run_problems(name, receipt):
   468	                notes.append(
   469	                    f"{goal_id}: superseded run receipt {name} no longer "
   470	                    "validates (noted; newest run governs)"
   471	                )
   472	
   473	        newest_name, newest = ordered[-1]
   474	        problems = run_problems(newest_name, newest)
   475	        if problems:
   476	            notes.append(f"{goal_id}: " + "; ".join(problems))
   477	            # the newest run governs: when it no longer validates we do
   478	            # NOT fall back to an older still-valid run
   479	            return notes + [
   480	                f"{goal_id}: newest run receipt does not validate — locked"
   481	            ]
   482	        best_run = newest
   483	
   484	        # (sha256, path) pairs, not bare hashes: identical bytes at two
   485	        # paths (latest.json vs a timestamped snapshot) must not let a
   486	        # verification "cite" an artifact the run receipt never named.
   487	        # Non-dict entries or ill-typed fields are skipped (never
   488	        # unhashable-tuple crashes): they already locked the goal above.
   489	        run_cited = {
   490	            (artifact.get("sha256"), artifact.get("path"))
   491	            for artifact in best_run.get("artifacts") or []
   492	            if isinstance(artifact, dict)
   493	            and isinstance(artifact.get("sha256"), str)
   494	            and isinstance(artifact.get("path"), str)
   495	        }
   496	        run_observed = _observed_at(best_run)
   497	
   498	        verifications = by_goal[KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION].get(goal_id) or {}
   499	        verified = None
   500	        for name, verification in sorted(verifications.items()):
   501	            problems = list(_receipt_blockers(
   502	                verification, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   503	                check_artifacts=_artifact_check_needed(verification),
   504	            ))
   505	            if verification.get("status") != "pass":
   506	                problems.append(
   507	                    f"verification {name} status={verification.get('status')!r}"
   508	                )
   509	            if not isinstance(verification.get("verifies"), str) or not (
   510	                verification.get("verifies")
   511	            ):
   512	                problems.append(f"verification {name} names no usable run id")
   513	            elif verification.get("verifies") != best_run.get("run_id"):
   514	                problems.append(
   515	                    f"verification {name} names run "
   516	                    f"{verification.get('verifies')!r}, not "
   517	                    f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}"
   518	                )
   519	            if not verification.get("artifacts"):
   520	                problems.append(
   521	                    f"verification {name} records no artifacts"
   522	                )
   523	            # EXACT identity match against the accepted independent lanes.
   524	            # A substring match would let e.g. "glm-codex" ride on "codex"
   525	            # and discharge a gate a corroborating lane must never open.
   526	            if verification.get("verifier") not in policy.get(
   527	                "independent_verifiers", []
   528	            ):
   529	                problems.append(
   530	                    f"verification {name} lane "
   531	                    f"{verification.get('verifier')!r} is corroborating, "
   532	                    "not independent"
   533	                )
   534	            # A verification cannot predate the run it claims to verify.
   535	            # (unparseable observed_at already produced a blocker above,
   536	            # so None never reaches this comparison)
   537	            v_observed = _observed_at(verification)
   538	            if v_observed is not None and v_observed < run_observed:
   539	                problems.append(
   540	                    f"verification {name} predates run "
   541	                    f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}"
   542	                )
   543	            # The verifier must have actually looked at the run's
   544	            # evidence: at least one verification artifact must reproduce
   545	            # an EXACT (sha256, path) pair the run receipt cites (run_id
   546	            # strings are reusable and identical bytes can live at many
   547	            # paths; the pair binds the verification to THIS run).
   548	            v_cited = {
   549	                (artifact.get("sha256"), artifact.get("path"))
   550	                for artifact in verification.get("artifacts") or []
   551	                if isinstance(artifact, dict)
   552	                and isinstance(artifact.get("sha256"), str)
   553	                and isinstance(artifact.get("path"), str)
   554	            }
   555	            if not (run_cited & v_cited):
   556	                problems.append(
   557	                    f"verification {name} cites none of run "
   558	                    f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}'s artifacts "
   559	                    "(exact sha256+path pairs)"
   560	                )
   561	            if problems:
   562	                notes.append(f"{goal_id}: " + "; ".join(problems))
   563	            else:
   564	                verified = verification
   565	                break
   566	        if verified is None:
   567	            return notes + [
   568	                f"{goal_id}: no current independent verification of run "
   569	                f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}"
   570	            ]
   571	        return []
   572	
   573	    satisfaction_cache: Dict[str, Tuple[bool, List[str]]] = {}
   574	
   575	    def goal_satisfaction(goal_id: str, seen: frozenset) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:
   576	        """(satisfied, blockers), recursion-safe on cycles. A goal is
   577	        satisfied only while its OWN evidence is current AND every
   578	        dependency is itself satisfied — own receipts can never unlock a
   579	        goal whose dependencies are still locked (cross-program MT-04 ->
   580	        MT-03 + FT-02 included)."""
   581	        if goal_id in satisfaction_cache:
   582	            return satisfaction_cache[goal_id]
   583	        if goal_id in seen:
   584	            return False, [f"{goal_id}: dependency cycle"]
   585	        if goal_id not in goals:
   586	            return False, [f"dependency {goal_id}: unknown goal id"]
   587	        blockers = list(own_evidence_blockers(goal_id))
   588	        for dep in goals[goal_id].get("depends_on") or []:
   589	            dep_satisfied, dep_blockers = goal_satisfaction(
   590	                dep, seen | {goal_id}
   591	            )
   592	            if not dep_satisfied:
   593	                blockers.append(f"dependency {dep} not satisfied")
   594	                blockers.extend(dep_blockers)
   595	        result = (not blockers, blockers)
   596	        satisfaction_cache[goal_id] = result
   597	        return result
   598	
   599	    for goal_id, goal in goals.items():
   600	        satisfied, sat_blockers = goal_satisfaction(goal_id, frozenset())
   601	        blockers: List[str] = list(sat_blockers)
   602	
   603	        eligible = True
   604	        if global_invalid:
   605	            # a receipt that cannot be attributed to ANY goal (unparseable
   606	            # JSON, missing goal_id) poisons the whole ledger: every goal
   607	            # locks until the file is repaired or removed — it must never
   608	            # be quietly dropped from the derivation
   609	            satisfied = False
   610	            eligible = False
   611	            blockers.append(
   612	                f"{goal_id}: evidence ledger has unattributable receipt(s) "
   613	                "— locked: " + "; ".join(global_invalid)
   614	            )
   615	        if goal.get("depends_on"):
   616	            for dep in goal["depends_on"]:
   617	                dep_satisfied, _ = goal_satisfaction(dep, frozenset())
   618	                if not dep_satisfied:
   619	                    eligible = False
   620	        else:
   621	            attestations = by_goal[KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION].get(goal_id) or {}
   622	            current_attestation = None
   623	            for name, attestation in sorted(attestations.items()):
   624	                a_blockers = _receipt_blockers(
   625	                    attestation, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   626	                    check_artifacts=False,
   627	                )
   628	                if a_blockers:
   629	                    blockers.extend(
   630	                        f"{goal_id}: attestation {name}: {b}" for b in a_blockers
   631	                    )
   632	                    continue
   633	                if attestation.get("status") == "pass":
   634	                    current_attestation = attestation
   635	                    break
   636	            if current_attestation is None:
   637	                eligible = False
   638	                blockers.append(
   639	                    f"{goal_id}: no current operator authorisation attestation"
   640	                )
   641	
   642	        evaluated.append(
   643	            {
   644	                "id": goal_id,
   645	                "title": goal.get("title"),
   646	                "program_id": goal.get("_program"),
   647	                "depends_on": goal.get("depends_on") or [],
   648	                "satisfied": satisfied,
   649	                "eligible": eligible,
   650	                "blockers": blockers,
   651	            }
   652	        )
   653	
   654	    return {
   655	        "ok": True,
   656	        "evaluated_at": now.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
   657	        "ledger_unattributable": global_invalid,
   658	        "policy": {
   659	            "receipt_ttl_days": policy.get("receipt_ttl_days"),
   660	            "independent_verifiers": policy.get("independent_verifiers"),
   661	        },
   662	        "programs": [
   663	            {
   664	                "program_id": r.get("program_id"),
   665	                "file": r.get("_file") or r.get("file"),
   666	                "sha256": r.get("_sha256"),
   667	                "canonical_source": r.get("owner_repository"),
   668	                "error": r.get("error"),
   669	            }
   670	            for r in registries
   671	        ],
   672	        "goals": evaluated,
   673	    }

exec

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Deterministic FT-00 Freqtrade dry-run guard and runner.
     2	
     3	Task card ``agent-tasks/inbox/finance-paper-trading-goals-2026-10-29.md``
     4	(canonical spec ``misumi/docs/core/finance-paper-trading-goals-v0.1.md``,
     5	goal FT-00: "Attest safe paper-only environment").
     6	
     7	The guard is fail-closed: a configuration is runnable only when EVERY
     8	safety property holds (simulation-only, spot, no credentials, no
     9	leverage/margin/derivatives, no public listeners, exactly the pinned
    10	official image, workspace outside the knowledgebase). Anything ambiguous
    11	is rejected. Overlay routes around the verified configuration —
    12	``add_config_files`` in the config, or extra ``--config``/``--userdir``/
    13	``--strategy`` freqtrade arguments — are rejected outright. The runner
    14	never sends anything but ``docker`` commands against the pinned image;
    15	there is no exchange credential path and no order routing other than
    16	Freqtrade's own simulated wallet.
    17	
    18	Deployment fact (observed live against the pinned digest 2026-10-29):
    19	a running freqtrade container recursively re-chowns the mounted
    20	user_data tree to its internal user (uid 1000 — a different account on
    21	the host), so the invoking user cannot keep write access to the
    22	workspace across runs. The verified-config snapshot write/cleanup is
    23	therefore DELEGATED to one-shot pinned-image containers (stdin -> file,
    24	and rm) whenever a direct write is not permitted, with the bytes
    25	re-read from the host afterwards to assert the round trip.
    26	
    27	Simulation only. This module must never be used to place real orders —
    28	real-money execution requires a separately ratified authority that this
    29	programme does not hold (see misumi finance framework).
    30	"""

    36	import json

    47	RUNNER_NAME = "finance-ft-runner"
    48	RUNNER_VERSION = "0.4"
    49	
    50	# Official image pinned by digest (tag ``stable`` resolved 2026-10-29,
    51	# image created 2026-09-29, Python 3.14.7 / CCXT 4.5.84). The guard
    52	# requires this reference EXACTLY: a digest alone proves nothing about
    53	# which repository it names, so ``evil/freqtrade@sha256:<same digest>``
    54	# must not pass, and a bare tag is never accepted — pinning must be by
    55	# digest so a re-pulled "stable" cannot silently change the engine under
    56	# an attestation.
    57	DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE = (
    58	    "freqtradeorg/freqtrade@sha256:"
    59	    "9d67afb8eb5f4e1210f4fb067efcbd5e005bd4f1c27f6f3888b8b9fc7f9b5199"
    60	)
    61	DIGEST_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9./_-]+@sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$")
    62	SPOT_PAIR_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Z0-9.]+/[A-Z0-9.]+$")
    63	TIMEFRAME_RE = re.compile(r"^\d+[smhd]$")
    64	TIMERANGE_RE = re.compile(r"^\d{8}(-\d{8})?(-)?$")
    65	STRATEGY_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$")
    66	CONFIG_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$")
    67	SHA256_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
    68	
    69	# Extra freqtrade arguments are restricted to this allowlist. Freqtrade
    70	# merges multiple --config files with later files taking precedence and
    71	# lets --userdir/--strategy/--db-url override verified values, so those
    72	# (and anything unrecognised) are refused: they could re-introduce
    73	# credentials, derivatives settings or listeners the guard just rejected.
    74	ALLOWED_EXTRA_FLAGS = {
    75	    "--dl-trades", "--erase", "--prepend",
    76	    "--timerange", "--days", "--new-pairs-days",
    77	}
    78	VALUE_EXTRA_FLAGS = {"--timerange", "--days", "--new-pairs-days"}
    79	
    80	# The TYPED data channel (--pairs/--timeframe/--timerange -> data_args) is
    81	# validated by the same grammar the CLI applies: these flags, these value
    82	# shapes. data_args reaches docker only through build_docker_argv, which
    83	# enforces this allowlist itself — a caller forgetting CLI validation must
    84	# not be able to smuggle an unvalidated token into the argv.
    85	DATA_FLAGS_BOOLEAN = {"--dl-trades", "--erase", "--prepend"}
    86	DATA_FLAGS_VALUE = {"--timeframe", "--timerange", "--days", "--new-pairs-days"}
    87	DATA_FLAGS_MULTI = {"--pairs"}
    88	DAYS_RE = re.compile(r"^\d+$")
    89	
    90	CONTAINER_NAME = "finance-ft00-dryrun"
    91	
    92	# Where the sha-bound entrypoint stages the verified config INSIDE the
    93	# container before freqtrade runs. Deliberately OUTSIDE the mounted
    94	# /freqtrade/user_data volume: the container's writable layer is not
    95	# reachable by any host-side process that can merely write the mounted
    96	# workspace, so the checksum and freqtrade's open() act on bytes no
    97	# workspace-writer can replace.
    98	PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH = "/tmp/ft-verified-config.json"

   100	# Exchanges usable with PUBLIC data only (no key required for OHLCV).
   101	ALLOWED_PUBLIC_SPOT_EXCHANGES = {"kraken", "binance"}
