/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba src/finance_goals.py | sed -n '1,360p'" in /home/agent/projects/odysseus-finance-r12
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Deterministic finance goal-lock evaluator (MT-xx / FT-xx).
     2	
     3	Task cards ``finance-market-media-intelligence-2026-10-29.md`` and
     4	``finance-paper-trading-goals-2026-10-29.md``; canonical goal registries
     5	live in ``tyecam1/misumi@main:config/finance-*-goals.json`` and are
     6	mirrored read-only into ``config/finance-goals/`` for the runtime.
     7	
     8	The registries are durable DEFINITIONS, not runtime state. This module
     9	derives current status on every call, fail-closed:
    10	
    11	- a goal is **satisfied** only while it has a ``run`` receipt with
    12	  status=pass and a ``run_id``, inside its TTL, whose recorded artifact
    13	  hashes still match the live files, AND a current
    14	  ``independent_verification`` receipt (status=pass, with its own
    15	  hash-checked artifacts) from an accepted independent verifier — matched
    16	  by EXACT identity, never substring — naming that exact run, AND every
    17	  dependency of the goal is itself satisfied (own evidence can never
    18	  unlock a goal whose dependencies are still locked);
    19	- a goal is **eligible** (may be worked on) when its unlock arm holds:
    20	  root goals need a current ``operator_attestation``; dependent goals
    21	  need every dependency satisfied;
    22	- anything absent, stale, future-dated, hash-mismatched, malformed or
    23	  verified only by a corroborating (non-independent) lane is a blocker —
    24	  locked, never assumed good. Evidence supersession is strict in BOTH
    25	  directions: the NEWEST run receipt governs (when it stops validating
    26	  the goal locks with no fallback to older runs), older receipts are
    27	  notes only, and a fresh run + a verification that cites one of the
    28	  run's own artifacts (exact sha256+path pair, dated at/after the run)
    29	  can re-satisfy the goal after the code or artifacts it cites change.
    30	  A receipt that cannot even be attributed to a goal (unparseable, no
    31	  goal_id) locks EVERY goal until the ledger is repaired.
    32	
    33	No scheduler, no writes, no network: a pure function of (registries,
    34	receipts, files, clock). The HTTP adapter in
    35	``routes/finance_goals_routes.py`` exposes it read-only.
    36	"""
    37	
    38	from __future__ import annotations
    39	
    40	import hashlib
    41	import json
    42	from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
    43	from pathlib import Path
    44	from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
    45	
    46	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    47	DEFAULT_REGISTRIES_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "config" / "finance-goals"
    48	DEFAULT_RECEIPTS_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "automation" / "review" / "finance" / "receipts"
    49	DEFAULT_POLICY_PATH = DEFAULT_REGISTRIES_DIR / "runtime-evaluation.json"
    50	
    51	KIND_RUN = "run"
    52	KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION = "operator_attestation"
    53	KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION = "independent_verification"
    54	VALID_STATUSES = {"pass", "fail", "insufficient"}
    55	
    56	DEFAULT_POLICY: Dict[str, Any] = {
    57	    "receipt_ttl_days": {
    58	        KIND_RUN: 7,
    59	        KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION: 365,
    60	        KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION: 30,
    61	    },
    62	    # Accepted independent verifier identities (standing rules: GLM lanes
    63	    # are corroborating only; Sol-class Codex or the human owner are the
    64	    # independent lanes that can discharge a gate).
    65	    "independent_verifiers": ["codex", "sol", "human:tye"],
    66	    "goal_ttl_overrides": {},
    67	}
    68	
    69	REQUIRED_RECEIPT_FIELDS = ("goal_id", "kind", "status", "observed_at")
    70	
    71	# A receipt dated more than this far in the FUTURE is rejected: clocks
    72	# drift, but a receipt that predates its own evaluation by months is
    73	# evidence tampering, not freshness.
    74	FUTURE_SKEW_MINUTES = 5
    75	
    76	
    77	class ReceiptError(ValueError):
    78	    """A receipt file is malformed — recorded as a blocker, never fatal."""
    79	
    80	
    81	def _parse_iso(value: str) -> datetime:
    82	    return datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    83	
    84	
    85	def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    86	    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    87	    with path.open("rb") as handle:
    88	        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(65536), b""):
    89	            digest.update(chunk)
    90	    return digest.hexdigest()
    91	
    92	
    93	def sha256_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
    94	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    95	
    96	
    97	def load_policy(path: Path = DEFAULT_POLICY_PATH) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    98	    policy = json.loads(json.dumps(DEFAULT_POLICY))
    99	    if path.exists():
   100	        try:
   101	            override = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   102	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   103	            return policy
   104	        for key in ("receipt_ttl_days", "independent_verifiers"):
   105	            if isinstance(override.get(key), dict):
   106	                policy[key].update(override[key])
   107	            elif isinstance(override.get(key), list):
   108	                policy[key] = override[key]
   109	        if isinstance(override.get("goal_ttl_overrides"), dict):
   110	            policy["goal_ttl_overrides"].update(override["goal_ttl_overrides"])
   111	    return policy
   112	
   113	
   114	def load_registries(directory: Path = DEFAULT_REGISTRIES_DIR) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   115	    registries = []
   116	    for path in sorted(directory.glob("finance-*-goals.json")):
   117	        try:
   118	            data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   119	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   120	            registries.append(
   121	                {"file": path.name, "program_id": path.stem, "error": str(exc),
   122	                 "goals": []}
   123	            )
   124	            continue
   125	        data["_file"] = path.name
   126	        data["_sha256"] = sha256_file(path)
   127	        registries.append(data)
   128	    return registries
   129	
   130	
   131	def load_receipts(directory: Path = DEFAULT_RECEIPTS_DIR) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   132	    receipts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   133	    if not directory.exists():
   134	        return receipts
   135	    for path in sorted(directory.glob("*.json")):
   136	        try:
   137	            data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   138	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   139	            receipts.append(
   140	                {"_file": path.name, "_invalid": f"unparseable: {exc}"}
   141	            )
   142	            continue
   143	        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   144	            receipts.append({"_file": path.name, "_invalid": "not an object"})
   145	            continue
   146	        # NEVER trust a receipt-supplied _file: the real file name is the
   147	        # ledger key downstream, and a spoofed one could collide with (and
   148	        # silently overwrite) another receipt's bucket entry
   149	        data["_file"] = path.name
   150	        # ill-TYPED values poison the same way missing ones do: a list
   151	        # goal_id/kind/status would otherwise raise mid-derivation and
   152	        # take the whole /goals response down instead of locking
   153	        missing = [
   154	            f for f in REQUIRED_RECEIPT_FIELDS
   155	            if not isinstance(data.get(f), str) or not data.get(f, "").strip()
   156	        ]
   157	        problems: List[str] = []
   158	        if missing:
   159	            problems.append(f"missing/ill-typed fields: {missing}")
   160	        elif data["status"] not in VALID_STATUSES:
   161	            problems.append(
   162	                f"status {data['status']!r} not in {sorted(VALID_STATUSES)}"
   163	            )
   164	        artifacts = data.get("artifacts")
   165	        if artifacts is not None and (
   166	            not isinstance(artifacts, list)
   167	            or any(not isinstance(a, dict) for a in artifacts)
   168	        ):
   169	            problems.append("artifacts must be a list of objects")
   170	        if problems:
   171	            data["_invalid"] = "; ".join(problems)
   172	        receipts.append(data)
   173	    return receipts
   174	
   175	
   176	def _ttl_days(receipt: Dict[str, Any], policy: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[int]:
   177	    goal_overrides = policy.get("goal_ttl_overrides") or {}
   178	    per_goal = (goal_overrides.get(receipt.get("goal_id")) or {}).get(receipt["kind"])
   179	    if isinstance(per_goal, int):
   180	        return per_goal
   181	    return (policy.get("receipt_ttl_days") or {}).get(receipt["kind"])
   182	
   183	
   184	def _receipt_blockers(
   185	    receipt: Dict[str, Any], *, now: datetime, policy: Dict[str, Any],
   186	    repo_root: Path, check_artifacts: bool,
   187	) -> List[str]:
   188	    """Fail-closed wrapper: ANY unexpected failure while evaluating a
   189	    hostile receipt is itself a blocker (the receipt cannot vouch for
   190	    anything) — it can never take the whole /goals response down. This
   191	    is deliberate: reviews repeatedly found new ill-typed-input crashes,
   192	    so the invariant is enforced structurally, not per-field."""
   193	    try:
   194	        return _receipt_blockers_inner(
   195	            receipt, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   196	            check_artifacts=check_artifacts,
   197	        )
   198	    except Exception as exc:  # broad by design: fail closed, never 500
   199	        return [
   200	            f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')!r}: could not be evaluated "
   201	            f"({type(exc).__name__}: {exc}) — locked"
   202	        ]
   203	
   204	
   205	def _receipt_blockers_inner(
   206	    receipt: Dict[str, Any], *, now: datetime, policy: Dict[str, Any],
   207	    repo_root: Path, check_artifacts: bool,
   208	) -> List[str]:
   209	    if receipt.get("_invalid"):
   210	        return [f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: {receipt['_invalid']}"]
   211	    blockers: List[str] = []
   212	    try:
   213	        observed = _parse_iso(receipt["observed_at"])
   214	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   215	        return [f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: observed_at not ISO-8601"]
   216	    if observed.tzinfo is None:
   217	        observed = observed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   218	    ttl = _ttl_days(receipt, policy)
   219	    if ttl is None:
   220	        blockers.append(
   221	            f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: unknown kind {receipt['kind']!r}"
   222	        )
   223	    else:
   224	        try:
   225	            expiry = observed + timedelta(days=ttl)
   226	        except OverflowError:
   227	            # 9999-12-31T23:59:59Z + ttl overflows datetime; a receipt
   228	            # stamped at the far future edge is future-dated, not a crash
   229	            expiry = None
   230	        if expiry is not None and now > expiry:
   231	            blockers.append(
   232	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: stale (observed "
   233	                f"{receipt['observed_at']}, ttl {ttl}d)"
   234	            )
   235	        elif expiry is None:
   236	            blockers.append(
   237	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: future-dated (observed "
   238	                f"{receipt['observed_at']} beyond datetime range; recovers "
   239	                "only if re-stamped within range)"
   240	            )
   241	        elif observed > now + timedelta(minutes=FUTURE_SKEW_MINUTES):
   242	            # fail-closed but not permanent: the receipt ages into
   243	            # validity once the clock reaches its stamped time, so a
   244	            # single mis-dated receipt can never lock a goal forever
   245	            blockers.append(
   246	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: future-dated (observed "
   247	                f"{receipt['observed_at']}, now "
   248	                f"{now.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()}; recovers when "
   249	                "the clock reaches the stamped time)"
   250	            )
   251	    if check_artifacts:
   252	        for artifact in receipt.get("artifacts") or []:
   253	            if not isinstance(artifact, dict):
   254	                blockers.append(
   255	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: malformed artifact entry"
   256	                )
   257	                continue
   258	            label = artifact.get("label")
   259	            recorded = artifact.get("sha256")
   260	            art_path = artifact.get("path")
   261	            if (
   262	                not isinstance(label, str) or not label
   263	                or not isinstance(recorded, str) or not recorded
   264	                or not isinstance(art_path, str) or not art_path
   265	            ):
   266	                # ill-typed (list/object/int) fields are malformed, not a
   267	                # crash: they must lock the goal, not lose the response
   268	                blockers.append(
   269	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: malformed artifact entry"
   270	                )
   271	                continue
   272	            if art_path.startswith("repo:"):
   273	                # a repo:-prefixed artifact must stay INSIDE the repo:
   274	                # resolve and confine, so a receipt can never point the
   275	                # verifier at arbitrary host files (a symlink planted
   276	                # inside the repo that resolves outside is confined away
   277	                # too). Only the hash verdict would leak, but confinement
   278	                # is the fail-closed posture (GLM corroborating read,
   279	                # round-13 finding 4).
   280	                root = Path(repo_root).resolve()
   281	                path = (repo_root / art_path[5:]).resolve()
   282	                if not path.is_relative_to(root):
   283	                    blockers.append(
   284	                        f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact path "
   285	                        f"escapes the repository: {art_path}"
   286	                    )
   287	                    continue
   288	            else:
   289	                path = Path(art_path)
   290	            if not path.exists():
   291	                blockers.append(
   292	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact missing: {art_path}"
   293	                )
   294	            elif not path.is_file():
   295	                # a well-typed path that names a DIRECTORY (or anything
   296	                # else non-regular) is a per-goal blocker, never an
   297	                # IsADirectoryError that loses the whole response
   298	                blockers.append(
   299	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact is not a "
   300	                    f"regular file: {art_path}"
   301	                )
   302	            else:
   303	                try:
   304	                    actual = sha256_file(path)
   305	                except OSError:
   306	                    blockers.append(
   307	                        f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact "
   308	                        f"unreadable: {art_path}"
   309	                    )
   310	                else:
   311	                    if actual != recorded:
   312	                        blockers.append(
   313	                            f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact hash "
   314	                            f"mismatch ({label})"
   315	                        )
   316	    return blockers
   317	
   318	
   319	def _artifact_check_needed(receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   320	    return receipt.get("kind") in (KIND_RUN, KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION)

   349	    # goal_id but cannot be classified lock that goal, and receipts that
   350	    # cannot even be attributed (unparseable, no goal_id) lock EVERY
   351	    # goal — a corrupted ledger must fail closed, not quietly drop the

   401	    def _order_key(receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:

   410	    def own_evidence_blockers(goal_id: str) -> List[str]:
   411	        """Blockers in this goal's OWN run/verification receipts.
   412	
   413	        The NEWEST run receipt (by observed_at) governs — valid or not.
   414	        If the newest run no longer validates (stale, future-dated,
   415	        artifact hash mismatch, unparseable timestamp), the goal is
   416	        LOCKED even when an older run still validates: fresh evidence

   438	        def run_problems(name: str, receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:

   573	    satisfaction_cache: Dict[str, Tuple[bool, List[str]]] = {}
   574	
   575	    def goal_satisfaction(goal_id: str, seen: frozenset) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:
   576	        """(satisfied, blockers), recursion-safe on cycles. A goal is
   577	        satisfied only while its OWN evidence is current AND every
   578	        dependency is itself satisfied — own receipts can never unlock a
   579	        goal whose dependencies are still locked (cross-program MT-04 ->
   580	        MT-03 + FT-02 included)."""
   581	        if goal_id in satisfaction_cache:
   582	            return satisfaction_cache[goal_id]
   583	        if goal_id in seen:
   584	            return False, [f"{goal_id}: dependency cycle"]
   585	        if goal_id not in goals:
   586	            return False, [f"dependency {goal_id}: unknown goal id"]
   587	        blockers = list(own_evidence_blockers(goal_id))
   588	        for dep in goals[goal_id].get("depends_on") or []:
   589	            dep_satisfied, dep_blockers = goal_satisfaction(
   590	                dep, seen | {goal_id}
   591	            )
   592	            if not dep_satisfied:
   593	                blockers.append(f"dependency {dep} not satisfied")
   594	                blockers.extend(dep_blockers)
   595	        result = (not blockers, blockers)
   596	        satisfaction_cache[goal_id] = result
   597	        return result
   598

   606	            # JSON, missing goal_id) poisons the whole ledger: every goal

   415	        artifact hash mismatch, unparseable timestamp), the goal is
   416	        LOCKED even when an older run still validates: fresh evidence
   417	        cannot silently fall back to a superseded result. Older receipts
   418	        are notes, surfaced only while the goal stays locked. A receipt
   419	        whose observed_at cannot be parsed is treated as newest (it
   420	        cannot be proven older), and a receipt that cannot be classified
   421	        at all locks the goal from the malformed bucket."""
   422	        malformed = malformed_by_goal.get(goal_id) or []
   423	        if malformed:
   424	            return [
   425	                f"{goal_id}: malformed receipt(s) in the evidence ledger — "
   426	                "locked: " + "; ".join(malformed)
   427	            ]
   428	
   429	        notes: List[str] = []
   430	        run_receipts = by_goal[KIND_RUN].get(goal_id) or {}
   431	        ordered = sorted(
   432	            run_receipts.items(),
   433	            key=lambda kv: (_order_key(kv[1]), kv[0]),
   434	        )
   435	        if not ordered:
   436	            return [f"{goal_id}: no run receipt"]
   437	
   438	        def run_problems(name: str, receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:
   439	            problems: List[str] = []
   440	            if receipt.get("status") != "pass":
   441	                problems.append(f"run receipt {name} status={receipt.get('status')!r}")
   442	            elif not isinstance(receipt.get("run_id"), str) or not receipt.get(
   443	                "run_id"
   444	            ):
   445	                # a non-string run_id (list/object) is unusable identity,
   446	                # and value-equality must never let an ill-typed pair
   447	                # match an ill-typed verification below
   448	                problems.append(f"run receipt {name} has no usable run_id")
   449	            elif not receipt.get("artifacts"):
   450	                problems.append(f"run receipt {name} records no artifacts")
   451	            else:
   452	                problems.extend(_receipt_blockers(
   453	                    receipt, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   454	                    check_artifacts=_artifact_check_needed(receipt),
   455	                ))
   456	            if _observed_at(receipt) is None:
   457	                problems.append(
   458	                    f"run receipt {name} has an unparseable observed_at — "
   459	                    "cannot be ordered against other evidence"
   460	                )
   461	            return problems
   462	
   463	        # Older receipts are history: their problems are noted but can
   464	        # neither lock nor unlock the goal. Only the NEWEST governs —
   465	        # and unparseable timestamps sort newest, so they lock.
   466	        for name, receipt in ordered[:-1]:
   467	            if run_problems(name, receipt):
   468	                notes.append(
   469	                    f"{goal_id}: superseded run receipt {name} no longer "
   470	                    "validates (noted; newest run governs)"
   471	                )
   472	
   473	        newest_name, newest = ordered[-1]
   474	        problems = run_problems(newest_name, newest)
   475	        if problems:
   476	            notes.append(f"{goal_id}: " + "; ".join(problems))
   477	            # the newest run governs: when it no longer validates we do
   478	            # NOT fall back to an older still-valid run
   479	            return notes + [
   480	                f"{goal_id}: newest run receipt does not validate — locked"
   481	            ]
   482	        best_run = newest
   483	
   484	        # (sha256, path) pairs, not bare hashes: identical bytes at two
   485	        # paths (latest.json vs a timestamped snapshot) must not let a
   486	        # verification "cite" an artifact the run receipt never named.
   487	        # Non-dict entries or ill-typed fields are skipped (never
   488	        # unhashable-tuple crashes): they already locked the goal above.
   489	        run_cited = {
   490	            (artifact.get("sha256"), artifact.get("path"))
   491	            for artifact in best_run.get("artifacts") or []
   492	            if isinstance(artifact, dict)
   493	            and isinstance(artifact.get("sha256"), str)
   494	            and isinstance(artifact.get("path"), str)
   495	        }
   496	        run_observed = _observed_at(best_run)
   497	
   498	        verifications = by_goal[KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION].get(goal_id) or {}
   499	        verified = None
   500	        for name, verification in sorted(verifications.items()):
   501	            problems = list(_receipt_blockers(
   502	                verification, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   503	                check_artifacts=_artifact_check_needed(verification),
   504	            ))
   505	            if verification.get("status") != "pass":
   506	                problems.append(
   507	                    f"verification {name} status={verification.get('status')!r}"
   508	                )
   509	            if not isinstance(verification.get("verifies"), str) or not (
   510	                verification.get("verifies")
   511	            ):
   512	                problems.append(f"verification {name} names no usable run id")
   513	            elif verification.get("verifies") != best_run.get("run_id"):
   514	                problems.append(
   515	                    f"verification {name} names run "
   516	                    f"{verification.get('verifies')!r}, not "
   517	                    f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}"
   518	                )
   519	            if not verification.get("artifacts"):
   520	                problems.append(
   521	                    f"verification {name} records no artifacts"
   522	                )
   523	            # EXACT identity match against the accepted independent lanes.
   524	            # A substring match would let e.g. "glm-codex" ride on "codex"
   525	            # and discharge a gate a corroborating lane must never open.
   526	            if verification.get("verifier") not in policy.get(
   527	                "independent_verifiers", []
   528	            ):
   529	                problems.append(
   530	                    f"verification {name} lane "
   531	                    f"{verification.get('verifier')!r} is corroborating, "
   532	                    "not independent"
   533	                )
   534	            # A verification cannot predate the run it claims to verify.
   535	            # (unparseable observed_at already produced a blocker above,
   536	            # so None never reaches this comparison)
   537	            v_observed = _observed_at(verification)
   538	            if v_observed is not None and v_observed < run_observed:
   539	                problems.append(
   540	                    f"verification {name} predates run "
   541	                    f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}"
   542	                )
   543	            # The verifier must have actually looked at the run's
   544	            # evidence: at least one verification artifact must reproduce
   545	            # an EXACT (sha256, path) pair the run receipt cites (run_id
   546	            # strings are reusable and identical bytes can live at many
   547	            # paths; the pair binds the verification to THIS run).
   548	            v_cited = {
   549	                (artifact.get("sha256"), artifact.get("path"))
   550	                for artifact in verification.get("artifacts") or []
   551	                if isinstance(artifact, dict)
   552	                and isinstance(artifact.get("sha256"), str)
   553	                and isinstance(artifact.get("path"), str)
   554	            }
   555	            if not (run_cited & v_cited):
   556	                problems.append(
   557	                    f"verification {name} cites none of run "
   558	                    f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}'s artifacts "
   559	                    "(exact sha256+path pairs)"
   560	                )
   561	            if problems:
   562	                notes.append(f"{goal_id}: " + "; ".join(problems))
   563	            else:
   564	                verified = verification
   565	                break
   566	        if verified is None:
   567	            return notes + [
   568	                f"{goal_id}: no current independent verification of run "
   569	                f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}"
   570	            ]
   571	        return []
   572	
   573	    satisfaction_cache: Dict[str, Tuple[bool, List[str]]] = {}
   574	
   575	    def goal_satisfaction(goal_id: str, seen: frozenset) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:
   576	        """(satisfied, blockers), recursion-safe on cycles. A goal is
   577	        satisfied only while its OWN evidence is current AND every
   578	        dependency is itself satisfied — own receipts can never unlock a
   579	        goal whose dependencies are still locked (cross-program MT-04 ->
   580	        MT-03 + FT-02 included)."""
   581	        if goal_id in satisfaction_cache:
   582	            return satisfaction_cache[goal_id]
   583	        if goal_id in seen:
   584	            return False, [f"{goal_id}: dependency cycle"]
   585	        if goal_id not in goals:
   586	            return False, [f"dependency {goal_id}: unknown goal id"]
   587	        blockers = list(own_evidence_blockers(goal_id))
   588	        for dep in goals[goal_id].get("depends_on") or []:
   589	            dep_satisfied, dep_blockers = goal_satisfaction(
   590	                dep, seen | {goal_id}
   591	            )
   592	            if not dep_satisfied:
   593	                blockers.append(f"dependency {dep} not satisfied")
   594	                blockers.extend(dep_blockers)
   595	        result = (not blockers, blockers)
   596	        satisfaction_cache[goal_id] = result
   597	        return result
   598	
   599	    for goal_id, goal in goals.items():
   600	        satisfied, sat_blockers = goal_satisfaction(goal_id, frozenset())
   601	        blockers: List[str] = list(sat_blockers)
   602	
   603	        eligible = True
   604	        if global_invalid:
   605	            # a receipt that cannot be attributed to ANY goal (unparseable
   606	            # JSON, missing goal_id) poisons the whole ledger: every goal
   607	            # locks until the file is repaired or removed — it must never
   608	            # be quietly dropped from the derivation
   609	            satisfied = False
   610	            eligible = False

   458	
   459	
   460	def dry_run_smoke(
   461	    *,
   462	    image_ref: str,
   463	    user_data_dir: Path,
   464	    strategy: str,
   465	    log_dir: Path,
   466	    config_name: str = "config-ft00.json",
   467	    config_sha256: Optional[str] = None,
   468	    cycles: int = 2,
   469	    ready_timeout_s: float = 180.0,
   470	    ready_markers: Optional[List[str]] = None,
   471	    post_ready_s: float = 20.0,
   472	    runner: Any = None,
   473	) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   474	    """Start/stop (and restart) the dry-run bot. Each cycle: start
   475	    detached, poll logs for ALL ready markers, dwell, ``docker stop``
   476	    (SIGTERM graceful, --stop-timeout 60), require exit 0."""
   477	    markers = ready_markers or DEFAULT_READY_MARKERS
   478	    log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   479	    runner = runner or subprocess
   480	    evidence: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   481	
   482	    for cycle in range(1, cycles + 1):
   483	        name = f"{CONTAINER_NAME}-c{cycle}"
   484	        log_path = log_dir / f"dryrun-cycle{cycle}.log"
   485	        argv = build_docker_argv(
   486	            "dry-run", image_ref=image_ref, user_data_dir=user_data_dir,
   487	            config_name=config_name, strategy=strategy, name=name,
   488	            detach=True, config_sha256=config_sha256,
   489	        )
   490	        started = runner.run(argv, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)
   491	        if started.returncode != 0:
   492	            return {
   493	                "ok": False,
   494	                "cycle": cycle,
   495	                "stage": "start",
   496	                "error": (started.stderr or started.stdout or "")[-2000:],
   497	                "evidence": evidence,
   498	            }
   499	        deadline = time.monotonic() + ready_timeout_s
   500	        ready_at: Optional[float] = None
   501	        output = ""
   502	        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
   503	            logs = runner.run(
   504	                ["docker", "logs", name], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30
   505	            )
   506	            if logs.returncode != 0 and "No such container" in (
   507	                logs.stderr or ""
   508	            ):
   509	                log_path.write_text(output, encoding="utf-8")
   510	                return {
   511	                    "ok": False, "cycle": cycle, "stage": "crashed_early",
   512	                    "error": "container exited before readiness; logs captured "
   513	                             f"so far in {log_path}",
   514	                    "run_stdout": (started.stdout or "")[-500:],
   515	                    "evidence": evidence,
   516	                }
   517	            output = (logs.stdout or "") + (logs.stderr or "")
   518	            if all(marker in output for marker in markers):
   519	                ready_at = time.monotonic()
   520	                break
   521	            time.sleep(5)
   522	        if ready_at is None:
   523	            stop = runner.run(
   524	                ["docker", "stop", "--time", "60", name],
   525	                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120,
   526	            )
   527	            runner.run(["docker", "rm", "-f", name],
   528	                       capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
   529	            output += _stop_trailer(stop.returncode, output)
   530	            log_path.write_text(output, encoding="utf-8")
   531	            return {
   532	                "ok": False,
   533	                "cycle": cycle,
   534	                "stage": "ready",
   535	                "missing_markers": [
   536	                    m for m in markers if m not in output
   537	                ],
   538	                "log": str(log_path),
   539	                "evidence": evidence,
   540	            }
   541	        time.sleep(post_ready_s)
   542	        final_logs = runner.run(
   543	            ["docker", "logs", name], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30
   544	        )
   545	        output = (final_logs.stdout or "") + (final_logs.stderr or "")
   546	        stop = runner.run(
   547	            ["docker", "stop", "--time", "60", name],
   548	            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120,
   549	        )
   550	        runner.run(["docker", "rm", "-f", name],
   551	                   capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
   552	        # The stop outcome is itself FT-00 acceptance evidence: append a
   553	        # trailer AFTER the container output so the COMMITTED log proves
   554	        # the SIGTERM stop result. Without it the log ends at a RUNNING
   555	        # heartbeat and a failed stop would look identical to a clean
   556	        # one (round-10 review finding 1).
   557	        output += _stop_trailer(stop.returncode, output)
   558	        log_path.write_text(output, encoding="utf-8")
   559	        evidence.append(
   560	            {
   561	                "cycle": cycle,
   562	                "ready_markers_seen": markers,
   563	                "stop_returncode": stop.returncode,
   564	                "log": str(log_path),
   565	                "log_tail": output[-500:],
   566	            }
   567	        )
   568	        if stop.returncode != 0:
   569	            return {
   570	                "ok": False, "cycle": cycle, "stage": "stop",
   571	                "error": (stop.stderr or "")[-2000:],
   572	                "evidence": evidence,
   573	            }
   574	    return {"ok": True, "cycles": cycles, "evidence": evidence}

## Verdict: FAIL

The round-14 host fix leaves a reproducible path for malformed domains to become healthy GDELT evidence.

1. **Medium — malformed hosts still count as publishers.** [finance_mt_probe.py](/home/agent/projects/odysseus-finance-r12/src/finance_mt_probe.py:298) checks only the *first character* of the top label and does not validate the other labels. `https://a.a-/x` resolves to `a.a-`, and `https://-.com/x` resolves to `-.com`. An in-memory GDELT probe gave each a healthy record; a repeated URL counted as an exact duplicate instead of a second malformed article. This is the phantom-record class the new commit claims to close.

2. **Low — repository confinement has a concurrent-swap gap.** [finance_goals.py](/home/agent/projects/odysseus-finance-r12/src/finance_goals.py:280) resolves and checks an artifact path before opening it for hashing at line 304. A concurrent workspace writer could replace an in-repo path with a symlink between those steps, causing a host file to be hashed. The stated traversal and *pre-existing* symlink cases are blocked.

3. **Low, accepted for FT-00 — strategy code remains mutable at execution.** [finance_ft_runner.py](/home/agent/projects/odysseus-finance-r12/src/finance_ft_runner.py:342) mounts `user_data`; the [cycle log](/home/agent/projects/odysseus-finance-r12/automation/review/finance/ft00/dryrun-cycle1.log:26) shows Freqtrade loading the strategy from that mount. A writer could swap its Python file after the receipt hash. With dry-run, spot-only configuration and no exchange credentials, this is an acceptable **simulation-only FT-00 residual**, but it remains work for FT-01.

**GLM dispositions:** 1 **partially fixed** (finding 1); 2 **fixed** (IPv4 literals now resolve `unknown`); 3 **refuted as claimed** (both Unicode and percent-encoded `bücher.de.` heal to `xn--bcher-kva.de`); 4 **fixed for traversal and existing symlinks**, subject to finding 2; 5 **accepted residual** (corrupt policy JSON returns the stricter defaults without changing response shape).

**Other checks:** All 5 MT and 10 FT artifacts in the two newest receipts hash-match `HEAD` via `git cat-file`. Older receipts appear only as superseded notes. The evaluator reports MT-00 and FT-00 **eligible but unsatisfied**, naming exactly `mt00-lab-20261106T164853Z` and `ft00-lab-20261106T1651Z` as lacking independent verification. Code and focused probes support exact verifier matching (`glm-codex` cannot pass), required run and verification artifacts, newest-run governance without revival, dependency locking, the fail-closed receipt wrapper, exact image pinning across the runner and snapshot helpers, and overlay rejection. Review of the collectors also supports the stated excluded-source, story-key, zero-evidence, HN ID/title, and unlocatable-host behavior.

**MT-00 health:** The newest run has HN **25/25** and Kraken **24 candles** healthy.
